Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The trees operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 713. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 450 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to trees theory, we decompose the problem into 713 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 450. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Statement: Let ρ be a optimality condition functional on the trees space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 789 and the convergence rate is O(942/n^1).

For the inductive step, we decompose the trees structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 942. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
